# PLAN — the single source of truth (projects · tasks · ETAs)

**This file is THE plan.** Every task lives here, under a project, with an `ETA` date and a `status`.
The long-horizon plan (the 6/22 launch) lives here too — as dated tasks, not a separate doc. There is no
second plan surface: the calendar is phone reminders only, the harness #-task list is a session scratch
mirror, `CHANGELOG.md`/git hold history. If it's planned, it's a line here.

**Line format** (strict — parsed by `scripts/check_plan.py`):
`- #<id> | <YYYY-MM-DD ETA> | <status> | <title>`   ·  status ∈ pending · in_progress · blocked

**The two rituals operate ONLY on this file:**
- **OPEN** (every session): run `python scripts/check_plan.py --today` → it prints OVERDUE + due-today open
  tasks. That list IS the day's plan. State it before reacting to anything.
- **CLOSE** (every session): every open task's ETA must be ≥ today. Rebump any past-due one to a real
  future date (or close it). File every new item as a line here under a project. Then commit — the
  pre-commit gate (`check_plan.py`) REFUSES the commit if any open task lacks a project, an ETA, or has a
  past ETA, or if an open harness task is missing from this file. No gaps, mechanically.

_Last CLOSE: 2026-06-16._

---

WETO +12.08R and FBRX +10.71R are +22.8R of +24.2R (94%). Ex-both: n=44, mean +0.03R. And BOTH thresholds sit just under those two trades' own coordinates — gap ≥15% is the highest cut keeping WETO (18.2%), vol ≥90th the highest keeping FBRX (91.8th). Across a 7×5 grid the ex-top-2 mean never exceeds +0.10R anywhere.** That is NOT 'no edge' — a tail strategy looks like this (healthy reference: 13% of trades ≥3R carrying +0.95R; this cell is 4/46 = 8.7%) — **but the tail RATE is unknown to within 5x at n=46, and resolving it is the shadow's ONLY job. Every gate counts TAIL EVENTS, not trades.** ▶ **Build: `mi_strategies` row (`phase='shadow'`, `min_median_r: null`) · `lowcap_lane.py` hooked into `run_ep_scan` BEFORE the shortlist cut (`:3543` — today `check_filters` sees only the top 20 and the prescore sorts small caps last, so this population has NEVER been seen by any study) · nightly `lowcap_lane_replay.py` walking from each row's OWN tick wall-clock (the sign flips at 09:36) · an `_ADAPTERS` entry or promotion returns 'no data' forever · boundary tests + a MAGNA53 BYTE-IDENTITY test (the hook sits on the live scan path in the ORB window — it must not move a single alert).** ⚠ **The registry's stock gate is WRONG IN BOTH DIRECTIONS here: `_eval_unpaired_r` uses the median, and with 37% of outcomes at +0.33 and 35% at −1.00 a `min_median_r: 0.0` would PASS a losing lane while the seeded 0.5 would BLOCK a winning tail lane forever.** ⚖ **Graduation binds to #545 (the ENTRY/EXIT TACTICS PROGRAM — note the name; Gemini called it 'limit-exit reversion' and the YAML predicate must cite the real task). Rationale: 37% of this cell's outcomes ARE the +2R-partial scratch, so if #545 changes the partial/exit mechanics the lane's edge is RE-PRICED, not merely delayed.** ⛔ **DO NOT waive the extension guard — 19 of the 46 were blocked by it, waiving it reverses his own 2026-08-29 signed revert, and the group ex-WETO is −0.014R. ⚠ **THE RANKING IS A PRIORITISATION, NOT AN ANSWER — three traps carry over from #556/#557 and must be re-applied per gate:** (1) these are RAW PEAKS from the gap-day open, not fills, and not R; (2) **gate ORDER censors everything** — the cascade is RVOL/pace (`ep_detector.py:~2939`) → cooldown (2953) → **extension (3017)** → quality: ADV → ATR% → mcap (3027, `backtester/filters.py::check_filters`) → score<50 (4045), so an EARLY gate's population was never tested against the later ones and its count is inflated by names that would have died anyway; extension is early, which is exactly why it tops this table; (3) do not condition on the outcome.
▶ **PER-GATE METHOD (proven twice): price it like `docs/analysis/adv_floor_556_2026-08-20.md` and `cooldown_cost_557_2026-08-21.md`** — unconditional median AND tail, the band a loosening would actually admit, survival against the REMAINING gates, and what the gate PREVENTED. ⚖ Every loosening is CHANGE_PROCESS + his sign-off; **loosen ONE at a time, smallest first (P9).**

  **(a) A FILLED position reported as SKIPPED for a full book.** He received *"⏭️ ORB skips: MRNA — Max open positions reached (5/5)"* and then, in the same batch, *"✅ FILLED: MRNA"*. Trace: MRNA filled **09:31:09 ET**; `unified_allocation_decided` ran at **09:35:00** and logged `open_positions: 5, slots_available: 0` — **with MRNA itself as one of the 5.** The second pass counted the position against its own candidacy and emitted a skip for a name we already own. It reads as a MISSED TRADE on a stock in the book. ▶ Fix: a candidate that is already an open position must render as *already held*, never as *max positions reached* — or be excluded from the skip digest entirely.
  **(b) A 327-MILLISECOND RACE reported as an unprotected position.** He received *"⚠️ Stop order CANCELLED: MRNA — Position unprotected (4 sh). Remediation runs at 4:05 PM ET — monitor."* The audit trail shows the entire partial sequence inside one third of a second: `partial_exit_stop_replaced` **09:55:00.801** (old 4-share stop cancelled, 3-share replacement placed) → `partial_exit_sell_placed` 09:55:01.098 (the 1-share OCO) → `partial_exit_breakeven_armed` **09:55:01.128**. The websocket handler saw the cancel and alarmed before the replacement landed. **It even reported '4 sh' — the PRE-partial count, from before the sell.** The position was never unprotected. ⚠ **THIS IS THE EXACT TRAP #566 RECORDED AT SHIP TIME** (*"`get_open_orders` HIDES the held OCO stop leg — a coverage watchdog reading it will false-alarm... the same wrong-reading class that started this task"*), and the 08-18 message merge fixed the sibling *"Stop replaced"* branch but NOT this *"unprotected"* one. ▶ Fix: extend the same `_agrees` positive-evidence suppression to the naked-position branch — the `partial_exit_stop_telegram_pending` row already exists and was written at 09:55:01.128.
  ⚖ **THE LINE — messaging only.** No change to any stop, size, order or safeguard. **The naked alert MUST still fire on a genuinely uncovered position** — suppression only on positive evidence that a replacement is in flight for this trade, and it must SPEAK on any doubt. That guard is the whole point of the alert; weakening it would be worse than the noise.
  ▶ **DoD:** (a) an already-open candidate never renders as a position-cap skip; (b) the naked branch suppresses on the same evidence the stop-replaced branch already uses; (c) a test proving a REAL uncovered position still alerts; (d) mutation-proven. Deploy `execution`. [The Real EP Plan — Exit surface]






## Apollo Trades dashboard (portfolio-app2)
- #706 | 2026-10-26 | deployed | **Live-trade reporting surfaces — his 10-21 stats for `/trades` and the Apollo Trades dashboard, plus the duplicate ORB skip message.** His words: *"A few stats I'd like to add to the /trades command and this should be in the apollo trades dash as well. 1) open positions -> position size as % of portfolio, stop % (% away current price is from stop, i.e. how much % we lose if get stopped) 2) closed positions -> along with R return and $ pnl, also % win/loss per trade. We can file and work on this later"*. Build: (1) OPEN positions — position size as % of the account's equity, and stop % = (current price − stop) ÷ current price (the % lost if stopped from here); (2) CLOSED positions — % win/loss per trade = (exit − entry) ÷ entry beside R and $ P&L; both in `/trades` (`channels/telegram.py:591` → the market agent's `/trades/summary`; orchestrator-owned, so `deploy.sh orchestrator`/`both`) and in the portfolio-app2 Apollo Trades tab (`apollo_data.py`; the repo is PUBLIC — no real values in fixtures or commits). (3) The duplicate ORB skip Telegram (10-21 SPCX twice, 10 s apart): `process_new_alerts_live` runs from the bar stream AND the 09:31 cron fallback (`broker/bar_stream.py:378`, `scheduler.py:1504`) and each sends its digest (`broker/live_tracker.py:796`); send a ticker's skip line once per day unless the reason changes — the second evaluation still runs (a recovered gap can still enter); message-only, no trading change; broker path → review + `deploy.sh execution` in a deploy window. DoD: `/trades` and the dashboard show both stats on every open and closed position, read against the broker's own numbers on one real position; and a day with a skip sends ONE skip line per ticker. WOULD-FAIL-IF: a stop % that ignores the side (a short's stop sits above the price), a % of portfolio computed on a stale equity, or two skip lines for one ticker and reason on a day after the fix. >> ⚠ **10-22 noon deploy did NOT ship** — `deploy.sh both` froze the prod box (3.8 GB RAM, no swap, two --no-cache builds) ~12:05–12:54 ET; he power-cycled it from the Hetzner console; all six containers came back healthy on the OLD images (4-day-old build); a 4 GB swapfile was added (his "ok"). Redeploy queued for tonight 21:15 ET, one service at a time (`deploy.sh market-agent`, then `deploy.sh execution`). >> 📝 **PRE-REGISTERED 10-22 17:25 PT, before tonight's 21:15 ET pull (baseline today: `/trades` shows R and $ P&L only; the SPCX skip line was sent twice on 10-21, 10 s apart).** EXPECT: (1) the RENDERED `/trades` reply (a code block) shows, on every open position, its size as a % of the account equity the endpoint reads and the stop % from the current price, side-aware, with '—' where the current price or stop is missing; on every closed position the % win/loss beside R and $; (2) on the first morning with ≥ 1 ORB skip, ONE skip line per (ticker, reason class) across the bar-stream and the 09:31 cron evaluations, while BOTH evaluations still log; (3) UNINTENDED — none expected: entry actions unchanged (same orders and skips), no Telegram parse error, the dashboard export gains side / current_price / market_value / account_equity. DONE-WHEN: the DoD's two reads — one rendered `/trades` read against the broker's own numbers on one real position, and one skip morning with one line per ticker. WOULD-FAIL-IF: a short's stop % computed as a long's, a % of portfolio on an equity snapshot older than the day, two skip lines for one ticker and reason on one ET day, or the second evaluation missing from the log (the dedupe ate the evaluation, not just the message). VERIFY-LIVE: grep `trade_stats` in apollo-market and the skip dedupe in apollo-execution; render `/trades/summary` from inside the container; the next skip morning's Telegram + the 09:31 log. >> ✅ **DEPLOYED 10-22 21:17–21:28 ET, one service at a time** (`deploy.sh market-agent` exit 0, then `deploy.sh execution` exit 0; server 0a76e61b; the box held 2.2–2.8 GB free throughout, swap ≤ 360 MB): `trade_stats.py` + `fmt_stat_line` in apollo-market, the skip dedupe (`_skip_digest_sent`) in apollo-execution. **Read 1 of the DoD MET the same night** — the RENDERED `/trades` summary (the market agent's `/trades_detail summary`, the path the Telegram command calls) against the broker's own numbers on the one live position: IRON 3 sh, $224.85 of $4,932.82 equity = '4.6% of equity' (broker market_value / equity), now $74.95 (broker current_price), stop $66.01 → '11.9% to stop' (long); closed rows carry the % win/loss (SMCI −3.5% = 35.27/36.54 − 1; KOD +47.1% = the share-weighted two legs). DONE-WHEN: the first real rejection/cancel is checked; if none by 2026-11-30, put closing it as unexercised to him.  🔴 **A live entry was rejected on a stock that ran +33%, and Alpaca had TOLD us why — we were discarding it on every rejection.** INSM 2026-08-06: buy 7 @ stop_limit 129.41/130.06 with an OTO stop at 126.15, submitted 13:31:11.892606Z, `failed_at` 13:31:11.896Z — **3.4 MILLISECONDS**, i.e. pre-trade validation, not a market event. The stock was +32.6% by 09:35 and +33.5% by 09:40. **ALPACA'S OWN WORDS, recovered:** `"reason": "[6098] Stop Price Already Triggered/Exceeds $ Threshold"`. **WHERE IT HID:** not on the order object (checked every key of the raw REST payload) — it is on the trade-updates EVENT beside the order, and the SDK's `TradeUpdate` model has NO `reason` field, so `raw_data=False` parses it away before our handler runs. Every rejection we have ever had arrived with a reason we threw out. ⚠ **Hours were spent inferring** — ruling out buying power ($17,355 vs a $910 order), account state, symbol restrictions, an in-the-money trigger, and a paper probe (`scripts/probes/_540_oto_rejection_probe.py`, 6 cases) that DID find a real rule (`stop_loss.stop_price must be <= base_price - 0.01`, base_price = the LIMIT price) which **does not explain INSM** (126.15 ≤ 130.05 passes). ⚠ **An external research pass proposed 'market price was below the stop-loss' — FALSIFIED by our own data**: last was $128.96, above the $126.15 stop. **SHIPPED:** `alpaca_client.fetch_broker_reject_reason()` — a targeted lookup against the trade-events history, called ONLY on reject/cancel/expire, never on the fill path; the reason lands in the audit detail as `broker_reason` AND in the operator Telegram. Deliberately NOT `raw_data=True`: that would rewrite every handler on the FILL path to work on dicts to recover a field needed only on a terminal failure. Never raises; a missing reason degrades silently. 7 tests. Proven against the real INSM order: returns the 6098 line. ▶ **VERIFY 8/07: the next broker rejection carries `broker_reason` in its audit row and its Telegram.** >> 🔴 **VERIFY 2026-08-07 FAILED — the capture returned NULL on the first real rejection since it shipped.** QNST was cancelled by the venue at 09:31:10 and the audit detail recorded **`"broker_reason": null`** — yet the reason was sitting right there on the event stream (`"Unsolicited: Bad Stop 19.8"`), and I retrieved it BY HAND from the same endpoint minutes later. So the mechanism is wired and the lookup does not find what a manual query finds. ⚠ **Do not close this on the INSM test** — that proved the function can read a `rejected` event after the fact; this is a `canceled` event, read live, and it came back empty. Candidate causes, none confirmed: the 4-second window is too tight for a 6 ms cancel whose event may not be queryable yet; or the handler filters on `rejected` and a `canceled` event never reaches the lookup. **DoD: the next broker cancel OR rejection carries a non-null `broker_reason` in its audit row and Telegram — proven on a real live event, not a replay.** This is the exact defect class the task exists to close: the broker told us why, twice, and both times we threw it away. only OKTA 09:49 came after). **Each of those six is counted TWICE — once inside `open_positions` consuming a slot, once as the winner of the slots that remain, so a flip at THIS TIMING would over-allocate. ⚠ That is an artifact of running AFTER the fills, not a sizing defect in the allocator — the timing move removes it.** The review's own comparison (audit `winners` vs actual fills) is therefore circular: agreement is guaranteed when the winners are drawn from names already filled. ⚠ Four months of telemetry answer nothing, and more days will not help — winner trade-through is **5 of 19 (26%)**, the same ~1/6 interception rate that deferred this on 2026-05-18, and on 7 of 9 contested days FEWER names entered than there were slots (39 candidates, 19 slots, 8 entries) so the 5-position cap is not what decides these books. ▶ **THE PRECONDITION: move the shadow job 9:35 → 9:28 ET and fix the double-count, shadow-only, submits nothing, $0** — then the FCFS-vs-allocator question becomes askable for the first time. ⛔ It changes WHEN a money-path job reads the book, so it ships with the numbers and **his sign-off (THE LINE)** — like the RED-3 clamp above, it must land BEFORE any Phase-1B flip. ✅ **The move was checked before being recommended: a 9:28 job does NOT read an empty queue.** Since #515 the only writer is `enqueue_pending_allocation` (`ep_detector.py:5999`) on an EP scan that runs every 5 min from 07:00 ET, and **19 of 27 queue rows across five sampled days were already present by 09:28**. ⚠ **Its cost, stated: the other 8 arrive AFTER 09:28** — post-open upgrades from the dedicated 9:31 `ep_scan_open` job — so a 9:28 allocator ranks the field EARLY but INCOMPLETE. The richer alternative is the 2026-07-03 doc's §9.3 (read slots live at each candidate's own attempt time), which keeps the full field and costs more. ✅ **HE RULED 2026-09-14: "aligned, A, then B if this becomes necessary" — SIGNED. Do A (move the shadow job to 9:28 ET + fix the double-count), and only build B (slots read live at each candidate's own attempt time) if A's incomplete-field cost shows up in the data.** ⚠ A is money-path TIMING, so it goes in a WEEKEND build slot (Sat 2026-09-19 or Sun 09-20), not a market day; it stays shadow-only and submits nothing. ⚠ **What would make B necessary, written down now so it is not decided after the fact: the post-9:28 arrivals turn out to be the ones that WIN the slots** — concretely, if over 15 contested post-move days the names entering after 09:28 would have outranked the 9:28 winners on more than a third of those days, A is judging the wrong field and B is owed. If they rarely outrank, A is sufficient and B is closed.  ⚠ #415 (allocator telemetry) shipped the eligibility flag and cascade info but its third piece, the operator's *"slots snapshot cadence"*, shipped as ONE value at the cron tick and closed on *"all 3 fields present"* (`731ea850`) — partially shipped, closed on the narrower reading; named as context, not reopened. 🔴 **MEASURED 2026-09-15 on his question (*"how do we determine priority of slots? it's based on score right?"*) — YES, AND THE +10 THEME BONUS IS 40% OF IT.** `ep_score` -> `score_magna53` -> `composite = 0.40*setup + ...` -> the allocator's rank. **Over 141 queued rows / 31 days: the cap actually bound on 14 days, and removing the +10 changes the WINNER SET on 2 of them (14%)** — 07-31 BLZE over MPWR (moot: both skipped downstream), 08-14 ETON over VERA (**ETON entered, +$19.32**; VERA was gap-below-floor anyway). **So it is NOT inert on the ranking side — but N=1 tradeable case is not a verdict either way.** ⚠ My first pass measured 12 scan-log HIGHs and got a clean null; the ranked population is the 141 queued rows, and on the right one the answer inverts — the same artifact as the 2026-07-19 "0 upgrades in 466 rows" read. **Re-measure after A lands, on post-move contested days.** §6 of the analysis doc. Round 3 = the weekend, after the refetch lands; then freeze, build, run. >> ✅ **10-23 both round-2 data blockers have a fix on the rebuilt 2017–21 file** (`apollo_data/cb702_pre_v2/`, see #705): duplicate companies 0 strict / 0 scaled pairs after the dedupe (alias map `dedupe_applied.tsv`); the refetch's recall per COMPANY = 98% of the known-gone list and 96% of the 808 liquid 2022 names absent by symbol (`scripts/probes/_705_survivorship_1023/05_recall_by_company.py`). The bar said '≥ 90% of the 415'; the 415's exact list was not reproduced, so it was judged on these two populations instead (282 and 808 names), both above 90%. Round 3 can freeze against v2. >> 📅 **CLOSE 10-23:** re-dated to Sun 10-25 (weekend build slot) — the parabolic-short replay round 3 against the rebuilt 2017–21 file (both round-2 data blockers now have a fix); the shadow stays event-gated (no climax candidate yet).
